# Database callsite cross-check

Independent of SQL keywords: indexes Go calls to Query/QueryRow/Exec/Begin/BeginTx/SendBatch/CopyFrom/Commit/Rollback and Python database execute/connect methods. Standard SQL at these sites is not automatically PostgreSQL-specific. Each entry inherits PG-01 (current adapter) and relevant behavior families from [11](11_source_evidence_catalog.md). The nearest function is a navigation hint. Tests are explicitly separated by path; nearest-function extraction is lexical, not a call graph. SQL spanning further lines remains in the linked source.
